- the canonical Playbook contract export now preserves that same doctrine semantically too: `exports/playbook.contract.example.v1.json` publishes `continuity_requirements.contract_role: "core_continuity_doctrine"` so export-only consumers can recover owner continuity identity without separate registry recall first.

- the continuity read surfaces now preserve that same doctrine pairing too: `pnpm playbook query runs --json` and `pnpm playbook session show --json` both publish additive `continuity.doctrine.role`, `path`, `export_path`, and fail-closed `registration_state`, so run/session readers no longer reconstruct doctrine identity separately.
- the bootstrap command family now preserves that same doctrine pairing too: `pnpm playbook ai-context --json`, `pnpm playbook context --json`, and `pnpm playbook ai-contract --json` each publish additive `continuity.doctrine.role`, `path`, `export_path`, and fail-closed `registration_state`, so AI bootstrap no longer needs a second doctrine lookup.
- the trusted repo-context path now preserves the same doctrine contract-plus-export pairing too: `pnpm playbook ask "what modules exist?" --repo-context --json` now includes `docs/contracts/PLAYBOOK-CONTRACT.md` plus `exports/playbook.contract.example.v1.json` in `repoContext.sources` and reports `repoContext.cacheLifecycle.shapeVersion: "2"`, so doctrine-carrying repo-context snapshots invalidate deterministically instead of reusing stale shape.
- the main machine-readable capture, retrieval, and promotion seams now preserve that same doctrine pairing too: `pnpm playbook memory --json`, `pnpm playbook knowledge --json`, and `pnpm playbook promote --json` now each publish additive `continuity.doctrine.role`, `path`, `export_path`, and fail-closed `registration_state`, including deterministic failure envelopes.

## Current Direction

2. Discord work lane
3. ATLAS systems lane

The supporting lane now sits at `10%` because the first read-only implementation slice is frozen, but it still remains downstream of the current held Sandbox truth until the command itself is routed and landed.
